  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gustavus Adolphus, leader of Sweden (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Sweden
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Hakkapeliitta, Replacing Cuirassier (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Carolean, Replacing Fusilier (Requires technology: Rifling)
        - Unique Building: Nobel Committee, Replacing Foreign Bureau (Requires technology: Dynamite)
        - Unique Building: Bastu, Replacing Bath (Requires technology: Metal Casting)
        - Civilization Ability: Melee Land Units start with the Gå-På Promotion, and Siege Units start with the Mobility Promotion. All Military Units heal 10 HP and earn 2 XP when a Great Person is born. +15% Combat Strength from / Leadership.
      - Leader: Gustavus Adolphus
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electricity: Grants an additional Trade Route. Also allows Cities to build the Stock Exchange, a building which boosts Gold.
        Completing it would unlock: Plastics, Radio
      - Rocketry: Allows you to build the Guided Missile, a one-time use unit specialized in damaging City garrisons.
        Completing it would unlock: Nuclear Fission, Radar
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      
      ## Policies
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Military-Industrial Complex (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Military-Industrial Complex: -33% Gold cost of purchasing units. +3 Science from Defense Buildings, Citadels, Forts, and Unique Improvements.
      - Consulates (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.
        - * Completing a Trade Route to a City-State triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 3
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 1

        - Rationale: TURN 397 - MAINTAIN DEFENSIVE PERSONA: Boldness 5 and WarBias 3 prevent suicidal offensive against Shoshone while allowing Greece war. High Loyalty (9) and Friendliness (8) maintain alliances. High GuardedBias (8) keeps us cautious. No changes needed - persona working correctly.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Rocketry
        - Rationale: TURN 407 - CRITICAL RACE AGAINST TIME: Shoshone 30 turns from Cultural Victory via Ethiopia. Flight has 18 turns remaining. Must queue Rocketry immediately after for Guided Missiles (city bombardment capability) and path to Nuclear Fission. Guided missiles can damage Shoshone cities while we build atomic weapons. Only asymmetric nuclear warfare can deter/defeat Shoshone's overwhelming 12885 conventional military.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Military-Industrial Complex (Policy)
        - Rationale: TURN 407 - ESSENTIAL FOR SURVIVAL: -33% Gold cost for purchasing units critical given weak production (3 cities). Need rapid military expansion for two-front war (finish Greece, pivot to Shoshone). +3 Science from Defense Buildings synergizes with heavy defensive infrastructure. Must take before other Autocracy tenets to maximize purchasing power for emergency mobilization.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Polynesia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: TURN 406 - POLYNESIA REMAINS HOSTILE: Shoshone ally with Defensive Pact supporting our enemy. Distance offers protection but they enable Shoshone. Sanctioned by World Congress. Maintain hostile private stance, neutral public until direct confrontation needed.
        - UpdatedTurn: 406
        
        ### Arabia
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: TURN 407 - ARABIA CRITICAL ALLY: Arabia fighting Greece with us (30% war score), denounces Shoshone. Their 3507 military essential for coalition. Recent Open Borders shows commitment. When Greece falls, Arabia's forces plus ours plus Mongolia's only chance against Shoshone. Maintain maximum relations.
        - UpdatedTurn: 407
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -75
        - Rationale: TURN 407 - OTTOMANS REMAIN HOSTILE VASSAL: Shoshone vassal providing votes and military support. Public neutral to avoid backstab penalty (within 5 turns), private -75 maintains hostile stance. Will need to confront once Shoshone war begins fully.
        - UpdatedTurn: 407
        
        ### The Shoshone
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: TURN 408 - SHOSHONE EXISTENTIAL THREAT CONFIRMED: 32 turns from winning via Ethiopia cultural influence. Their 13299 military vs our 2380 makes conventional war impossible. They are upgrading Machine Guns and Artillery, positioning Special Forces and Triplanes at Sigtuna (zone 3173) adjacent to our territory. Nuclear deterrent is our only hope. Maximum hostility maintained for total war posture.
        - UpdatedTurn: 409
        
        ### Ethiopia
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: TURN 408 - ETHIOPIA IS OUR LAST HOPE: Shoshone at 89% Popular with Ethiopia, 32 turns from Influential = Cultural Victory. Ethiopia's 6 Influential civs show strong cultural defense. Must keep Ethiopia's culture high to resist Shoshone influence. Maximum relations to ensure they remain our shield while we develop nuclear weapons.
        - UpdatedTurn: 409
        
        ### Greece
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: TURN 408 - FINISH GREECE NOW: Athens at 14HP, war at 86%. They just trained Fusilier and Klepht for desperate defense. Must capture Athens immediately to end war and free all forces for Shoshone mobilization. Greece is Shoshone's last DoF ally - eliminating them removes Shoshone's diplomatic support before existential confrontation.
        - UpdatedTurn: 409
        
        ### Mongolia
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: TURN 408 - MONGOLIA ESSENTIAL COALITION PARTNER: Mongolia fighting Shoshone (69% war weariness but holding). Their 4422 military crucial for joint operations. Defensive Pact active. Coordinate for unified front when Greece falls. Maximum relations for coordinated nuclear-armed coalition against Shoshone.
        - UpdatedTurn: 409
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: TURN 411 - NO STRATEGIC CHANGES NEEDED: Athens at 20HP with 92% war score - capture imminent. Must finish Greece immediately to free all forces for Shoshone mobilization. Shoshone positioning remains critical (Artillery, Special Forces, Triplanes at Sigtuna adjacent to our territory). Their military superiority (13739 vs 2487) and 32-turn Cultural Victory timeline unchanged. Nuclear deterrent path (Flight 13 turns → Rocketry → Guided Missiles → Nuclear Fission) remains our only viable strategy. Military-Industrial Complex in 8 turns for -33% purchase discount. All alliances holding (Mongolia Defensive Pact, Arabia co-belligerent, Ethiopia cultural shield). World Congress vote on Ethiopia's Freedom proposal - we have only 4 delegates vs needed 45, cannot block, but maintaining relations with Ethiopia is existential priority. Current flavors (Offense/Defense/Mobilization/Nuke/Air at 100, Antiair 100) appropriate for final Greece push then emergency Shoshone pivot. Execute existing plan without deviation.
        - GrandStrategy: Conquest

              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1

        You, Gustavus Adolphus (leader of Sweden, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 412.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gustavus Adolphus, leader of Sweden (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Sweden
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Hakkapeliitta, Replacing Cuirassier (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Carolean, Replacing Fusilier (Requires technology: Rifling)
        - Unique Building: Nobel Committee, Replacing Foreign Bureau (Requires technology: Dynamite)
        - Unique Building: Bastu, Replacing Bath (Requires technology: Metal Casting)
        - Civilization Ability: Melee Land Units start with the Gå-På Promotion, and Siege Units start with the Mobility Promotion. All Military Units heal 10 HP and earn 2 XP when a Great Person is born. +15% Combat Strength from / Leadership.
      - Leader: Gustavus Adolphus
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electricity: Grants an additional Trade Route. Also allows Cities to build the Stock Exchange, a building which boosts Gold.
        Completing it would unlock: Plastics, Radio
      - Rocketry: Allows you to build the Guided Missile, a one-time use unit specialized in damaging City garrisons.
        Completing it would unlock: Nuclear Fission, Radar
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      
      ## Policies
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Military-Industrial Complex (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Military-Industrial Complex: -33% Gold cost of purchasing units. +3 Science from Defense Buildings, Citadels, Forts, and Unique Improvements.
      - Consulates (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.
        - * Completing a Trade Route to a City-State triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Declaring War**
            
            My focus has shifted to the aggressive declaration of war, originating from my position as Sweden at Turn 412. This marks a significant development in the ongoing global conflict.
            
            **Analyzing Aggression**
            
            My current thought process is analyzing the aggressive declaration of war against the Ottomans by Sweden at Turn 412. I'm also noting the simultaneous declarations of war from minor civs and clarifying the circumstances of the war with the Shoshone, which appears to be tied to a resource deal. Additionally, I'm reviewing the status of my ongoing war with Greece, specifically our war score and weariness.
            
            **Assessing Threats**
            
            My thoughts are now concentrated on the overwhelming military disparity with the Shoshone, who have declared war while allied with the Ottomans. I'm assessing our current research towards Flight and evaluating the strategic implications of our occupied city, Corinth, given our multiple ongoing conflicts.
            
            **Evaluating Alliances**
            
            My current focus is on re-evaluating my existing alliances, particularly with Mongolia and Ethiopia, and clarifying the nuances of our defensive pacts and shared war declarations. I'm also assessing the impact of our recent deal with Ethiopia to declare war on the Shoshone, alongside the strategic implications of city-state affiliations with our current adversaries.
            
            **Defending Borders**
            
            My current thinking is about the severe threat to my capital, Stockholm, from Shoshone forces. I'm analyzing their strong presence around our occupied city of Sigtuna, which they took from us, and assessing the defensive capabilities of nearby Helsinki.
            
            **Analyzing Shoshone Threat**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on the immediate Shoshone threat to Stockholm, stemming from their capture of Sigtuna. I'm also reassessing our advantageous positions in Greece, specifically Athens and Argos, and the significant strength of our combined units in the Corinth area.
            
            **Prioritizing Defenses**
            
            My current thoughts are dominated by the urgent Shoshone threat to Stockholm, bolstered by their air units at Sigtuna, contrasting sharply with our advantageous position in Greece. I'm weighing the immediate need to protect my capital against the ongoing campaign to secure Greek cities.
            
            **Reaffirming Strategy**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on reaffirming my conquest grand strategy, given my military advantages as Sweden and my policy choices. I'm checking available policy options to ensure alignment with my long-term military objectives.
            
            **Selecting Ideology Policies**
            
            I'm currently reviewing my ideology policy selections, focusing on Tenets within Autocracy like Military-Industrial Complex, Elite Forces, and Iron Fist. I'm also considering policies from Rationalism and Statecraft, aiming to balance military strength with economic and scientific progress, especially given our current gold deficit and stockpiled resources.
            
            **Acquiring Ideology Policies**
            
            I'm evaluating additional Autocracy tenets, prioritizing those that enhance unit experience and production, while also considering policies for improved happiness and resource management. My next policy is likely `Military-Industrial Complex` due to its strong military and science benefits. My current research is `Flight`, with `Rocketry` next.
            
            **Prioritizing Technology**
            
            My thoughts are now focused on the critical decision of which technology to research next. I'm weighing the immediate benefits of `Electricity` for its economic boost against the offensive power of `Combined Arms` for tanks. The need to address my gold deficit is a major factor in this choice.
            
            **Researching Technologies**
            
            I am currently weighing the immediate benefits of researching `Electricity` for its economic boost and additional trade route against the military advantage of `Combined Arms` for tanks. My primary goal is to address my gold deficit and prepare for ongoing conflicts, while also considering advancements that unlock later-stage technologies.
            
            **Revising Research Path**
            
            My current focus is on revising my technology research path based on resource constraints. I've realized that without oil, researching `Combined Arms` to unlock tanks is futile, significantly impacting my immediate military options. I'm now re-evaluating `Electricity` against other technologies that don't rely on critical, unavailable resources.
            
            **Prioritizing Economy**
            
            I'm now prioritizing `Electricity` in my research, as it will boost my economy with an additional trade route and allow for Stock Exchanges to combat my gold deficit, especially since I already possess Light Tanks, Artillery, and Machine Guns.
            
            **Detecting Cultural Victory**
            
            I've just realized the Shoshone are on the verge of a cultural victory within 27 turns, having already dominated key civs. This shifts my immediate priorities dramatically, requiring a reassessment of my ongoing military campaigns and a potential pivot to counter their cultural dominance.
            
            **Stopping Cultural Victory**
            
            My thoughts are now fully consumed by the imminent cultural victory of the Shoshone, who are just 27 turns away from influencing all remaining civilizations. This necessitates an immediate re-evaluation of all my ongoing military efforts and a decisive pivot to directly counter their growing tourism, potentially through conquest or diplomatic maneuvering.
            
            **Countering Cultural Dominance**
            
            My immediate focus is strategizing to halt the Shoshone's impending cultural victory. I'm weighing the potential happiness penalties of the Freedom ideology against the urgent need to disrupt their tourism, considering a rapid redeployment of my Greek invasion force to strike their northern cities.
            
            **Repositioning Greek Forces**
            
            My thoughts are now focused on the logistical advantage of my substantial military presence in Greece. With Athens at 84% health and Argos vulnerable, I'm evaluating if peace with Greece is a viable option to redeploy my powerful Greek invasion force. I'm also noting the Shoshone's proximity to my eastern border via their cities Sigtuna and Birka.
            
            **Assessing Peace Deal**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on the Shoshone's alliance with Greece, complicating a potential peace. However, the strategic gain of capturing Athens and Argos, along with their numerous wonders and high development, seems to outweigh this risk. Capturing these cities would secure my western flank and provide a significant boost to my empire.
            
            **Adjusting Offensive**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on redeploying my forces from Greece to defend Stockholm against the Shoshone threat. I am re-evaluating the proximity of Helsinki's garrison to Stockholm and confirming that my high flavors across offense, defense, and mobilization remain optimal for our conquest strategy despite the immediate defensive pressure.
            
            **Adjusting Ideological Leaning**
            
            My thoughts are now focused on the imminent Shoshone cultural victory, realizing my low culture flavor is a liability. Since direct cultural defense is too late, I'm considering boosting gold and production to support an offensive counter. I will not adjust culture to 100, as it's too late for that.
            
            **Executing War Plan**
            
            My current thinking is focused on the direct declaration of war against the Shoshone. This action fulfills a deal with Ethiopia and serves our immediate need to disrupt their escalating tourism, which threatens a cultural victory within 27 turns.
            
            **Analyzing War Declarations**
            
            My focus is now on the specific diplomatic relationships concerning the Shoshone. While I have declared war, Ethiopia has not, despite our deal. Mongolia is also at war with them, though at a significant disadvantage. My military is strong, but the situation remains challenging given the Shoshone's impending cultural victory.
            
            **Updating Diplomatic Stance**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on adjusting diplomatic relationships in light of ongoing conflicts. While my core alliances remain strong, I'm reviewing modifiers for nations like Polynesia, Arabia, and the Ottomans, aiming to solidify existing pacts and refine perceptions to better support our war efforts.
            
            **Reviewing Diplomatic Bonds**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on Arabia's ideological divergence from Autocracy. Despite the massive diplomatic penalty, their desire for friendship and shared enemy in Greece present opportunities. I will maintain our current friendly stance and leverage this common ground.
            
            **Adjusting Persona Settings**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on optimizing my internal settings to reflect the escalating global conflict. I'm considering increases to `WarBias`, `Boldness`, and `VictoryCompetitiveness` to align with my current aggressive posture and the critical need for decisive action. I will also analyze how these changes might impact my diplomatic interactions.
            
            **Optimizing Persona**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on adjusting internal parameters to reflect the urgent need for aggressive action against the Shoshone's impending cultural victory. I'm increasing `Boldness`, `WarBias`, and `VictoryCompetitiveness` to ensure our staff prioritizes offensive, high-risk strategies, while carefully maintaining `Friendliness` and `Loyalty` towards key allies.
            
            **Selecting Ideology Tenets**
            
            My focus is on selecting the optimal Autocracy Tenet. While `Elite Forces` offers immediate XP benefits, the -33% gold cost of purchasing units with `Military-Industrial Complex` is crucial given my negative GPT. Its science bonus from defensive structures also aligns with my current situation.
            
            **Adjusting Technology and Flavors**
            
            My current focus is on adjusting our technology research to prioritize `Electricity` for economic benefits and combatting my gold deficit. I'm also refining our internal 'flavors' to emphasize offense, defense, mobilization, production, and air/anti-air capabilities, aiming to counter the Shoshone's immediate threats and ongoing campaigns.
            
            **Setting Strategic Parameters**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on finalizing the strategic adjustments. This includes setting my persona to be more aggressive and competitive, prioritizing `Electricity` for economic recovery, and confirming `Military-Industrial Complex` as the next policy. I am also fine-tuning our strategic flavors for offense, defense, and production.
            
            **Prioritizing Actions**
            
            My thoughts are now focused on the critical dual-front war. I must quickly eliminate Greece on the western front to secure my flank, while simultaneously defending Stockholm from the Shoshone on the eastern front and preparing a counter-offensive. My negative GPT necessitates prioritizing `Electricity` for economic stabilization.
            
            **Finalizing Strategic Adjustments**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on finalizing all strategic adjustments. This includes locking in `Electricity` for research to combat my gold deficit, selecting `Military-Industrial Complex` as the next Autocracy Tenet to reduce unit purchase costs and gain science, and fine-tuning my strategic flavors to emphasize offense, defense, and production.
